Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对Wavelet卷积（WTConv）参考实现严重受限于高带宽内存（HBM）数据移动的问题，建立了WTConv的I/O模型来刻画该瓶颈，并提出三种代数重构：(1)在芯片上重算廉价Haar分析蝶形变换；(2)将多级合成级联折叠为以输出坐标位索引的单一闭式通行；(3)将学习到的逐通道缩放因子折叠入卷积权重。这些重构促成了I/O感知的融合实现，大幅减少HBM流量，在保持相同算术量的情况下，将模型化HBM流量降低约2.55倍，训练速度最高提升4.35倍，峰值内存占用减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WTConv作为标准卷积的即插即用替代品日益流行，能以线性参数增长实现指数级感受野扩展，但其参考实现因过度依赖高带宽内存（HBM）数据传输而严重受内存带宽限制，导致实际效率远低于其理论价值。作者希望消除这一系统开销瓶颈，释放WTConv的实际潜力。

Method: 作者首先建立WTConv的I/O模型以定量刻画内存瓶颈，随后提出三种代数重构来减少HBM流量：(1)对廉价的Haar分析蝶形变换采用在芯片上重算的方式避免内存往返；(2)将多级合成级联折叠为单个闭式表达式，以输出坐标位作为索引，消除中间存取的层级间数据移动；(3)将可学习的逐通道缩放系数合并进卷积权重，减少额外的内存访问。最终将这些重构整合为I/O感知的融合实现。

Result: 在WTConvNeXt配置及多种分解层级和张量形状上评估显示：参考WTConv在算术量相当的情况下仍显著慢于其所替代的深度卷积。经过重构后，模型化HBM流量降低约2.55倍，训练速度最高提升4.35倍，峰值内存占用约为原来的一半。

Conclusion: 本文的代数重构与I/O感知融合实现在保留WTConv全部优势（指数级感受野、线性参数增长）的前提下，大幅减少了执行时间和内存占用，消除了此前限制其实际效率的系统开销，使WTConv成为更可行的实用方案。

Abstract: Wavelet convolution (WTConv) has emerged as an increasingly popular drop-in replacement for standard convolutions, expanding a network's receptive field exponentially with the number of decomposition levels while keeping the parameter count linear. However, its reference implementation is severely memory-bound due to excessive data movement through high-bandwidth memory (HBM). We develop an I/O model of WTConv to characterize this bottleneck and use it to guide three algebraic reformulations: (1) recomputing the inexpensive Haar analysis butterfly on chip, (2) collapsing the multi-level synthesis cascade into a single closed-form pass indexed by output-coordinate bits, and (3) folding learned per-channel scales into the convolution weights. Together, these reformulations enable an I/O-aware fused implementation that substantially reduces HBM traffic. We evaluate the WTConvNeXt configuration across decomposition levels and a broad range of tensor shapes. Despite performing comparable arithmetic, the reference WTConv is substantially slower than the depthwise convolution it replaces. Our reformulation reduces modeled HBM traffic by approximately $2.55\times$, yielding up to a $4.35\times$ training speedup over the reference while roughly halving peak memory usage. Thus, our reformulation preserves the benefits of WTConv while substantially reducing its execution time and memory footprint, removing the systems overhead that previously limited its practical efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [StreamFlow: Dynamic Memory Flows for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10949)
*Muxin Fu,Yifan Zhang,Wentao Zhang,Fangming Guo,Qian Chen,Guibin Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamFlow是一个高效的视觉记忆框架，通过结合轻量级动态感知中期记忆（在视觉编码前过滤时间冗余）和潜在长期记忆（将历史视频内容整合为可供后续推理访问的视觉潜在表示），配合注意力引导的检索机制，在流式视频理解中实现动态按需访问历史视觉信息。在StreamingBench上达到67.73%的整体准确率，视觉注意力分数提升59.1%，端到端延迟降低50.4%，峰值内存降低21.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法存在两方面的局限：基于模型的方法需要对骨干网络进行侵入式更新，而基于记忆的方法在时间冗余内容上耗费大量视觉编码计算，且对视觉历史采用刚性的访问方式。这促使研究者探索一种既能高效过滤冗余、又能动态灵活访问历史视觉信息的框架。

Method: StreamFlow由三个核心组件构成：(1) 轻量级dynamics-aware中期记忆，在视觉编码之前过滤时间冗余；(2) 潜在长期记忆，将历史视频内容整合为可供推理访问的视觉潜在表示；(3) 注意力引导的检索机制，在生成阶段当模型对视觉证据的依赖减弱时，动态注入相关视觉潜在表示。

Result: 在StreamingBench上达到67.73%的整体准确率，达到流式视频理解的最优性能；同时在线长视频基准上也表现优异。相对于vanilla设置，视觉注意力分数提升59.1%，端到端延迟降低50.4%，峰值内存降低21.1%，实现了更强的视觉接地能力和更高的推理效率。

Conclusion: StreamFlow通过"编码前过滤冗余+潜在表示压缩历史+注意力引导按需检索"的三级设计，在严格的因果性和有限内存约束下同时兼顾了流式视频理解的准确性、视觉接地性和推理效率，为流式多模态理解提供了高效可行的视觉记忆方案。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to preserve relevant evidence from continuously evolving streams under strict causality and bounded memory. Yet existing paradigms remain limited: model-based methods require intrusive backbone updates, while memory-based methods expend substantial visual-encoding computation on temporally redundant content and rely on rigid access to visual history. To address these limitations, we introduce StreamFlow, an efficient visual memory framework that enables dynamic, on-demand access to historical visual information. StreamFlow combines a lightweight, dynamics-aware mid-term memory that filters temporal redundancy before visual encoding with a latent long-term memory that consolidates historical video content into visual latents accessible to subsequent reasoning. During generation, an attention-guided retrieval mechanism injects relevant visual latents when the model's reliance on visual evidence weakens. StreamFlow achieves state-of-the-art streaming video understanding performance, reaching 67.73% overall accuracy on StreamingBench, while also delivering strong performance on offline long-video benchmarks. Relative to the vanilla setting, it improves the visual attention score (VAS) by 59.1% while reducing end-to-end latency and peak memory by 50.4% and 21.1%, respectively, enabling more visually grounded and efficient reasoning.

</details>


### [71] [Modelling Geographic Atrophy Progression using Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10807)
*Simone Sarrocco,Paul Friedrich,Florentin Bieder,Christina Bornberg,Philippe Valmaggia,Peter Maloca,Philippe Cattin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用隐式神经表示（INRs）对年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）的地理萎缩（GA）进展进行个体化建模，可在低数据环境下同时生成过去和未来时间点的眼底自发荧光（FAF）图像和GA分割结果。该方法在GA病灶面积的MAE和DICE分数上优于对比模型，且不牺牲FAF图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期AMD（干性）以不可逆的地理萎缩（GA）为特征，其进展具有高度个体化差异，目前对其演化过程的理解仍有限。纵向FAF图像是评估病灶生长的主要工具，但如何对GA的个体化进展进行建模仍是一个未解问题，尤其在低数据场景下。

Method: 利用隐式神经表示（INRs）在个体水平上建模GA进展。该方法以低数据设置为前提，输入纵向FAF图像，同时生成过去和未来时间点的FAF图像及其对应的GA分割结果。

Result: 在对比模型中，该方法在不同场景下取得了具有竞争力的分割质量：GA病灶面积上取得最低的平均绝对误差（MAE），DICE分数最高，同时未牺牲FAF图像生成质量。

Conclusion: 基于INRs的方法能够有效建模个体化GA进展，在低数据条件下同时实现高质量的FAF图像生成和GA分割预测，为理解晚期AMD的进展机制提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Age-related Macular Degeneration (AMD) is the major cause of blindness in the Western world. Its late dry phase is characterised by irreversible atrophic areas, namely Geographic Atrophy (GA). Longitudinal Fundus Autofluorescence (FAF) image acquisitions are currently the main tool for assessing lesion growth over time at the image level. However, due to its highly individualised progression, the evolution of late AMD remains poorly understood. In this work, we propose using Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) to model GA progression at the individual level in a low-data setting. Our approach generates both FAF and GA segmentation at both past and future time points. Among the comparison models, our method achieves competitive segmentation quality across different scenarios, yielding the lowest Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for the GA lesion area and the highest DICE score, without sacrificing FAF image quality. The code is available at https://github.com/SimoneSarrocco/ga-progression-with-inrs.

</details>


### [72] [MultiModal Code-Switching: Interleaving Visual Objects into Language for Explicit Object-Level Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11167)
*Changhao Xiang,Shangyu Xing,Zhen Wu,Jianbing Zhang,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMCS（多模态语码转换），一种新型多模态预训练范式，通过将文本实体替换为对应视觉对象来实现显式的对象级监督，解决现有MLLM中图像级对齐导致的指称歧义问题。仅用50K样本即可匹配或超越用600K图像-文本对训练的模型，跨模型尺度一致提升视觉定位和感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM主要依赖图像-文本对进行模态对齐预训练，将全局图像表示映射到长文本描述。但这种图像级对齐存在指称歧义：模型难以从全局表示中推断多个视觉对象与文本实体之间的对应关系，导致数据效率低下和语义定位不佳。

Method: 提出MMCS（MultiModal Code-Switching）预训练范式。借鉴语言学中的语码转换现象，通过将文本实体替换为对应的视觉对象来交错视觉和语言编码，强制进行局部视觉-语言对齐。同时开发可扩展的数据合成流程，生成包含773K样本且具有精确对象-实体对应关系的预训练数据集。

Result: MMCS具有很高的数据效率：仅用50K样本即可匹配或超越用600K图像-文本对训练的模型。此外，MMCS在不同模型规模下一致地提升了视觉定位和感知能力。

Conclusion: 通过提供显式的对象级监督而非仅依赖全局图像级对齐，MMCS有效解决了MLLM预训练中的指称歧义问题，显著提升数据效率和语义定位性能。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on image-text pairs for modality alignment pretraining, mapping global image representations to long textual descriptions. However, this image-level alignment suffers from referential ambiguity: models struggle to infer the correspondences between multiple visual objects and textual entities from the global representation, leading to data inefficiency and suboptimal semantic grounding. To address this, we propose MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS), a novel pretraining paradigm that provides explicit object-level supervision. Inspired by the linguistic phenomenon of code-switching, MMCS interleaves vision and language by replacing textual entities with their corresponding visual objects, enforcing local vision-language grounding. We further develop a scalable data synthesis pipeline to generate a pretraining dataset of 773K samples with accurate object-entity correspondences. Experiments show that MMCS is highly data-efficient: with only 50K samples, it matches or surpasses models trained on 600K image-text pairs. Furthermore, MMCS consistently improves visual grounding and perception capabilities across varying model scales.

</details>


### [73] [MIRA: Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10827)
*Shengzhi Wang,Jun Yang,Kai Wu,Xiaozhong Ji,Yiwen Ye,Ziyang Chen,Mingliang Xiong,Wen Fang,Mingqing Liu,Mengyuan Xu,Miaoxuan Shan,Caiyan Liu,Bin He,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRA是一个医学视觉诊断框架，通过动态调用图像处理工具和网络搜索，并验证证据的相关性与一致性，实现自主证据搜索和反思性验证。采用两阶段训练策略：工具增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索数据引擎构建监督微调轨迹，强化学习通过在线反思性原理演化优化决策。在九个医学视觉推理基准上平均得分64.73，较Qwen3-VL-8B提升7.44分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉代理虽可利用工具检查图像和检索外部知识，但无差别地使用工具可能引入噪声或误导性证据。可靠诊断不仅需要获取额外观察，还需验证工具行为是否必要，以及所得证据是否支持当前假设，因此需要一个能够反思性验证的医学诊断框架。

Method: MIRA采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段，利用工具增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索数据引擎，探索多样化诊断假设，联合验证视觉定位准确性和语义一致性，构建监督微调轨迹；第二阶段，通过强化学习改进决策，利用在线反思性原理演化——将失败案例提炼为候选原理，仅保留能提升留出集rollout奖励的原理。

Result: 在九个医学视觉推理基准上，MIRA平均得分64.73，比Qwen3-VL-8B骨干提升7.44分；有用的工具使用判断从56.2%提升至73.8%，有害判断从8.9%降至1.6%。定性分析显示MIRA能够重新审视证据、纠正过早结论并调整工具使用策略。

Conclusion: MIRA通过反思性验证机制有效减少了工具使用的噪声证据，显著提升了医学视觉诊断的准确性和工具使用的有效性，展示了在医学诊断场景中自主证据搜索与反思验证相结合的优势。

Abstract: Medical visual agents can use tools to inspect images and retrieve external knowledge, but indiscriminate tool use may introduce noisy or misleading evidence. Reliable diagnosis therefore requires not only acquiring additional observations, but also verifying whether tool actions are necessary and whether the resulting evidence supports the current hypothesis. We introduce MIRA (Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis), a medical visual diagnostic framework for autonomous evidence search and reflective verification. MIRA dynamically invokes image-processing operations, including zooming, grounding, pointing, rotation, and measurement, as well as web search, while evaluating the relevance and consistency of the acquired evidence. We develop MIRA through a two-stage training strategy. First, a tool-augmented Monte Carlo Tree Search data engine explores diverse diagnostic hypotheses and jointly verifies visual grounding accuracy and semantic consistency to construct supervised fine-tuning trajectories. Second, reinforcement learning further improves decision-making through online reflective principle evolution: failure cases are distilled into candidate principles, and only principles that improve held-out rollout rewards are retained. Across nine medical visual reasoning benchmarks, MIRA achieves an average score of 64.73, improving its Qwen3-VL-8B backbone by 7.44 points. It also increases useful tool-use judgments from 56.2% to 73.8% and reduces harmful judgments from 8.9% to 1.6%. Qualitative analyses show that MIRA can re-examine evidence, correct premature conclusions, and adapt its tool-use strategy. Project page: https://MIRA-VL.github.io/

</details>


### [74] [Test-Time Self-Evolving GUI Visual Grounding via Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11191)
*Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个测试时自进化框架（Test-Time Self-Evolving framework），用于GUI视觉定位任务。该框架通过探索（Exploration）、评估（Evaluation）、反思（Reflection）和内化（Internalization）的闭环机制，使GUI代理模型在部署后无需人工标注就能不断适应未见界面。核心创新包括基于MLLM的Reflector进行结果评估与反思，以及反思引导的在线策略自蒸馏（Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation）将高层推理转化为密集的token级监督信号，并配合对比校准（Contrastive Calibration）防止错误的自回归前缀污染监督信号。在六个基准上平均提升了7.4%的准确率，是首个将在线策略自蒸馏用于GUI视觉定位测试时适应的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉定位模型在部署后参数冻结，无法适应未见界面。虽然最近有方法尝试通过测试时强化学习进行适应，但这些方法无法对失败的探索进行反思，导致适应能力受限。本文旨在填补部署后适应的空白，使GUI代理具备完整的自我进化能力。

Method: 构建"探索-评估-反思-内化"的闭环框架：1) 探索阶段：代理预测未见界面的定位坐标进行探索；2) 评估阶段：引入基于MLLM的Reflector评估生成结果并提供推理反思；3) 内化阶段：提出反思引导的在线策略自蒸馏（Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation），通过条件自教师将高层次推理转化为密集的token级监督；4) 校准阶段：设计对比校准（Contrastive Calibration）方法，防止失败探索中错误的自回归前缀污染监督信号。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上进行了大量实验，相比基础模型平均准确率提升了7.4%，验证了框架的有效性。这是首个成功将在线策略自蒸馏应用于GUI视觉定位测试时适应的工作。

Conclusion: 通过填补部署后自适应的空白，该框架使GUI代理具备完整的自我进化能力，在无需人工标注的情况下实现了对未见界面的持续适应，代码将公开发布。

Abstract: GUI Visual Grounding is a fundamental capability for GUI agents. Existing models typically freeze their parameters after deployment, limiting their ability to adapt to unseen interfaces. Although recent methods attempt to adapt models via test-time reinforcement learning, they cannot reflect upon failed exploration. To overcome this, we propose a Test-Time Self-Evolving framework that enables models to improve after deployment without human-annotated ground truth. It constructs a closed-loop of Exploration, Evaluation, Reflection, and Internalization. Specifically, the agent first explores unseen interfaces by predicting grounding coordinates for given instructions. To evaluate these explorations, we introduce an MLLM-based Reflector to assess the generated results and provide the corresponding reasoning reflections. To internalize reflection knowledge into the model weights, we propose Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, which translates high-level reasoning into dense token-level supervision via a conditioned self-teacher. Furthermore, we design a Contrastive Calibration method to prevent incorrect auto-regressive prefixes from corrupting the supervisory signals during failed explorations. Extensive experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate our framework's effectiveness, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 7.4% over the base model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to successfully exploit on-policy self-distillation for test-time adaptation in GUI visual grounding. By filling the gap in post-deployment adaptation, our framework completes the self-evolving capability of GUI agents. The code will be released.

</details>


### [75] [UniProbe: A Learnable Token-Level Hallucination Detector for Large VLMs using Multi-Structural Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10835)
*Dvir Samuel,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Ethan Fetaya,Yftah Ziser,Gal Chechik,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniProbe是一种轻量级、统一的可学习检测器，通过建模冻结LVLM的单次前向计算轨迹（构建图结构编码图像块、查询令牌和生成令牌间的注意力关系），采用GNN、ViT和GRU交替的模块分别捕捉关系、空间和序列证据，实现SOTA的token级和对象幻觉检测，并在解码时可减少高达55%的对象幻觉，仅增加1.06倍延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）常产生与视觉输入不符的幻觉内容。现有检测方法要么需要昂贵的全模型微调，要么依赖忽略生成过程的外部验证器，要么将内部信号简化为孤立的特征和手工统计，丢失空间、序列和关系结构。因此需要一种无需微调主干模型、能充分利用异构计算轨迹中完整结构信息的轻量级检测器。

Method: UniProbe从冻结LVLM的单次前向传播中提取异构计算轨迹，构建有向图连接图像块、查询令牌和生成令牌，以注意力权重编码它们之间的关系。检测器采用交替的结构感知模块处理该轨迹：GNN建模关系证据，ViT处理2D视觉几何，GRU捕捉响应顺序。此外提出流式变体用于幻觉感知解码（检测并重采样幻觉令牌），以及自适应策略使检测器与LVLM自身生成对齐。

Result: 在多种LVLM主干上，UniProbe在token级和对象幻觉检测上均达到当前最优性能。在解码过程中，可将对象幻觉降低高达55%，而延迟仅为标准生成的1.06倍。

Conclusion: UniProbe通过统一建模LVLM计算轨迹中的空间、关系与序列信息，以轻量级可学习方式实现了高效的幻觉检测与缓解，在性能、效率和泛化性之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve impressive visual reasoning and dialogue capabilities, yet frequently hallucinate content unsupported by the visual input. Effective mitigation requires token-level localization, enabling targeted intervention without discarding the entire response. Existing detectors require expensive full-model fine-tuning, rely on external verifiers that ignore the model's generation process, or reduce internal signals to isolated features and hand-crafted statistics, discarding spatial, sequential, and relational structure. We introduce \textbf{UniProbe}, a lightweight, unified, learnable detector that models a frozen LVLM's heterogeneous computational trace from a single forward pass. UniProbe constructs a directed graph over image patches, query tokens, and generated tokens, with attention weights encoding their relations. It processes this trace with alternating structure-aware modules: a GNN for relational evidence, a ViT for 2-D visual geometry, and a GRU for response order. Interleaving them allows spatial, relational, and sequential evidence to interact throughout the detector. We further develop a streaming variant for hallucination-aware decoding, which detects and resamples hallucinated tokens during generation, and a self-adaptation strategy aligning the detector with the LVLM's own generations. Across diverse LVLM backbones, UniProbe achieves state-of-the-art token-level and object-hallucination detection. During decoding, it reduces object hallucinations by up to 55\% at $1.06\times$ the latency of standard generation.

</details>


### [76] [PolyLayout: Hierarchical VLM-Guided Layout Generation Beyond Rectangular Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10838)
*Yutong Jiang,Zahra Atashgahi,Carlos Soto Garcia Delgado,Ruben Brokkelkamp,Davide Zanutto,Efşan Sökmen,Shahin Shahkarami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向家居零售的混合分层3D房间布局生成框架，将生成过程解耦为三个层级：功能家具聚类与区内精细摆放、基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的宏观路由以锚定密集区域和独立家具于不规则多边形边界内、以及基于规则的碰撞消除与建筑约束合规的微观优化。在量产级目录和真实不规则拓扑上评估，该方法在感知合理性、几何合规性和延迟之间取得最佳平衡，并支持现有方法无法处理的非矩形边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术研究与真实零售应用之间存在鸿沟：现有家具布局方案主要关注算法层面（如家具摆放策略），严重忽视了真实住宅中普遍存在的非矩形几何形状以及严格的门窗约束，而这些对家居零售（如客户在家可视化产品并做购买决策）至关重要。

Method: 提出混合分层框架，将布局生成解耦为三个阶段：(1) 功能家具聚类与区域内精细摆放；(2) 利用VLM驱动的宏观路由，将聚类区域和独立家具锚定到多样的多边形边界内；(3) 基于规则的优化，实现无碰撞的微观布局并满足建筑约束。

Result: 在生产规模目录和代表性不规则真实拓扑上评估，方法在感知合理性上达到最高，同时保持良好几何合规性和较低延迟，并可扩展到现有方法无法原生支持的不规则边界。

Conclusion: 该混合分层框架有效弥合了学术研究与零售应用之间的差距，能够在不规则真实空间（非矩形、带门窗约束）中生成物理上合理且感知可信的3D房间布局，且具备生产级可扩展性。

Abstract: Generating physically plausible 3D room layouts is essential for home furnishing retail, enabling customers to visualize products in their own homes and confidently make purchasing decisions. However, a gap exists between academic research and real-world application: existing solutions primarily focus on algorithmic strategies for furniture placement, largely neglecting the non-rectangular geometries and strict door/window constraints prevalent in real homes. To bridge the gap, we introduce a hybrid, hierarchical framework tailored for retail, specifically designed to support scalable spatial planning applications. Our system decouples generation into three stages: (1) functional furniture clustering and fine-grained intra-zone placement; (2) macro-routing guided by a vision-language model (VLM) to anchor both these clustered zones and any remaining standalone furniture within diverse polygonal boundaries; and (3) rule-based optimization for collision-free micro-arrangements that respect architectural constraints. We evaluate our system on production-scale catalogs and a representative set of irregular real-world topologies. Our results show that our approach attains the highest perceptual plausibility while maintaining good geometric compliance at relatively low latency, and extends to irregular boundaries that existing methods do not natively support.

</details>


### [77] [The GENEA Challenge 2026: A Large-Scale Disentangled Evaluation of Speech-Driven Gesture Generation on the Seamless Interaction Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10839)
*Rajmund Nagy,Silvia Arellano García,Hendric Voss,Mihail Tsakov,Taras Kucherenko,Youngwoo Yoon,Gustav Eje Henter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第四届GENEA挑战赛的结果，对五个基于语音驱动的姿态生成系统进行大规模人工评估。研究采用了分离评估方法分别评估动作质量和语音对齐，并引入了新的语义姿态生成任务。通过四项大规模用户研究收集了超过23,000票，结果显示真实数据集的运动质量远高于所有参赛系统，但参赛系统在语音对齐、对话响应和语义表达方面均与真实数据存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音驱动姿态生成系统是虚拟角色动画和对话系统的重要技术。随着该领域快速发展，需要建立系统化、可复现的评估方法，尤其需要将运动质量与语音对齐效果分离评估，避免两者混淆。此外，现有评估缺乏对对话情境下聆听与回应他人效果的考察，也缺乏语义姿态生成能力的评测。

Method: 研究者使用Seamless Interaction对话数据集的dyadic对话，让五个团队训练语音驱动姿态生成系统。采用解耦评估方法分别评估动作质量和语音对齐；通过dyadic不匹配研究隔离聆听和回应的影响；新增语义姿态生成任务和文本不匹配评估方法（使用Grounded Gestures子集）。共进行四项大规模用户研究，收集来自869名测试者的超过23,000票。

Result: 运动真实感研究：数据集的过滤片段运动质量显著高于所有参赛系统（68-95%成对胜率）。语音对齐研究：动捕片段的概念天花板为62%对齐分数，最佳提交仅32%，其余仅略高于预期为0%的输入无关系统。dyadic研究中动捕设定了65%的天花板，但没有提交显著高于随机水平，说明系统无法回应对话者。语义不匹配评估中数据集高度表达性（测试者79%能识别匹配文本），但几乎所有提交都未能生成语义表达性的动作，最佳仅8%的适当性分数。

Conclusion: 第四届GENEA挑战赛表明，尽管语音驱动姿态生成领域持续进步，但当前系统与真实人类动作数据之间仍存在巨大差距：系统在语音对齐、对话响应和语义表达方面均远未达到真实水平。该挑战赛的投票和输出已公开，以促进可复现性和进一步研究。

Abstract: This preprint presents the results of the fourth GENEA Challenge, a large-scale human evaluation of five speech-driven gesture-generation systems trained by participating teams on the Seamless Interaction dataset of dyadic conversations. As in the 2023 GENEA Challenge, we used a disentangled evaluation methodology to assess motion quality and speech alignment without confounding between the two, and performed a dyadic mismatching study to isolate the effect of listening and reacting to the interlocutor. We additionally introduce a new semantic gesture-generation task and a text-mismatching evaluation methodology using the Grounded Gestures subset of the data. In total, we ran four large-scale user studies, collecting over 23,000 votes from 869 test-takers.
  In the motion-realism study, the dataset's filtered segments had substantially higher motion quality than all challenge submissions (68-95% pairwise winrate). In the speech-alignment study, the motion-capture segments provided a conceptual ceiling at 62% alignment score, with the top submission significantly behind at 32% and the rest only slightly above the 0% expected of an input-independent system. In the dyadic study, motion capture again set the ceiling at 65% appropriateness score, but no submission scored substantially above chance, indicating that the systems could not yet respond to the interlocutor. Finally, the semantic mismatching evaluation found highly expressive gestures in the dataset (test-takers identified the matching transcript 79% of the time), yet almost all submissions failed to generate semantically expressive motion, with the best achieving only an 8% appropriateness score.
  The collected votes and outputs will be made publicly available at https://genea-workshop.github.io/2026/challenge/ to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [78] [Multi-View Relational Distillation for Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10864)
*Kiet T. Nguyen,Hanbo Shim,Jinwoo Kim,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多视角关系蒸馏（MVRD），通过蒸馏跨视角的patch级余弦相似度（而非教师特征本身）来增强视觉语言模型（VLM）的几何空间推理能力。该方法在多个代表性VLM上提升了视觉空间推理性能，优于监督微调和特征蒸馏，且以更少的参数和更低的延迟接近特征融合方法，同时保持了视觉与文本表示的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在图像和视频理解上表现强大，但其视觉-空间表示在几何上存在脆弱性，导致在具身AI、机器人和自动驾驶等空间推理任务上失败。现有方法要么通过空间问答微调VLM（可能强化虚假空间表示），要么融合大规模几何视觉模型的特征（显著增加推理时模型规模），而直接蒸馏教师特征会破坏视觉与文本表示的预训练对齐，损害目标与语言语义能力。

Method: 提出多视角关系蒸馏（MVRD）：不直接匹配多视角教师特征，而是蒸馏跨视角的patch-wise余弦相似度。这些关系编码了空间理解所需的几何对应信息，同时使学生表示保持欠约束（underdetermined），从而能保持在预训练的视觉-语言空间附近。通过这种关系级蒸馏，避免了对特征空间的过度约束，在增强几何能力的同时保留原有的语言对齐。

Result: 在多个代表性VLM上，MVRD提升了视觉空间推理能力，优于监督微调和特征蒸馏方法；以显著更少的参数和更低延迟逼近特征融合方法的性能。MVRD使视觉表示更具几何性同时保持语言对齐，并能泛化到3D场景理解任务，包括目标接地（object grounding）、密集描述（dense captioning）和视觉问答。

Conclusion: 多视角关系蒸馏是一种有效的VLM几何接地方法，通过蒸馏关系而非特征，在保持预训练视觉-语言对齐的同时增强了空间推理能力，实现了参数效率和性能之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong image and video understanding, yet their visual-spatial representations remain geometrically fragile, leading to failures in spatial reasoning needed for embodied AI, robotics, and autonomous driving. Prior approaches to geometry grounding either fine-tune VLMs on spatial question answering, which can perpetuate spurious visual representations, or fuse features from large geometry-grounded vision models, which substantially increases model size at inference. Knowledge distillation from geometry-grounded vision models offers an alternative, but directly matching multi-view teacher features can disrupt the pretrained alignment between visual and textual representations, degrading object- and language-semantic capabilities. We propose multi-view relational distillation (MVRD), which distills patch-wise cosine similarities across views instead of the teacher features themselves. These relations encode geometric correspondences adequate for spatial understanding, while leaving the student representation underdetermined, allowing it to remain close to its pretrained vision- language space. Across representative VLMs, MVRD improves visual-spatial reasoning, outperforming supervised fine-tuning and feature distillation while approaching feature fusion methods with considerably fewer added parameters and lower latency. We show that MVRD makes visual representations more geometric while retaining language alignment, and generalizes to 3D scene understanding tasks such as object grounding, dense captioning, and question answering.

</details>


### [79] [NullEdit: Stealthy Image Protection via VLM Condition Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10870)
*Weiyao Huang,Liqin Wang,Ziqi Sheng,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NullEdit是一种针对现代图像编辑模型的推理时防御方法，通过将VLM表示重定向到无编辑锚点，在保持输出自然和源保留的同时抑制有害编辑，实现隐蔽的"空操作"防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像编辑工具虽功能强大，但也使未授权图像操作成为可能。现有推理时防御要么通过明显破坏暴露保护机制，要么允许编辑发生导致身份或内容漂移，无法真正阻止编辑行为本身。需要一种既隐蔽又能从根本上抑制有害编辑的防御方法。

Method: NullEdit针对VLM从参考图像和指令联合形成的表示进行干扰，在表示条件化下游DiT骨干网络之前将其重定向。利用正常编辑和無编辑锚点来引导表示重定向，并通过跨提示梯度平均将保护泛化到未见过的指令。

Result: 在Step1X-Edit和Qwen-Image-Edit上对CelebA-HQ和VGGFace2数据集进行实验，NullEdit相比SOTA基线平均将EditReward IF分数降低0.813，同时保持主体身份和源内容不变。

Conclusion: NullEdit实现了隐蔽且无害的防御效果：请求的编辑被抑制，输出保持自然和源保留，无可察觉的伪影或身份替换，且恶意指令要求的有害语义不会出现。

Abstract: Modern image editors combine vision-language models (VLMs) with diffusion transformer backbones to modify a single reference image according to instructions without fine-tuning. This capability also enables unauthorized manipulation of publicly released images. Existing inference-time defenses either invalidate edits through conspicuous corruption, thereby exposing the protection, or allow them to proceed with identity or reference content drift, thereby failing to prevent the editing behavior itself. We instead target a stealthy and harmless no-op in which the requested edit is suppressed, the output remains natural and source-preserving without conspicuous artifacts or identity replacement, and harmful semantics requested by malicious instructions are absent. We propose NullEdit, which targets the VLM representation jointly formed from the reference image and instruction before it conditions the downstream DiT backbone. Using normal-edit and no-edit anchors, NullEdit redirects this representation, while cross-prompt gradient averaging transfers protection to held out instructions. Across Step1X-Edit and Qwen-Image-Edit on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2, NullEdit reduces the EditReward IF score by 0.813 on average relative to the SOTA baseline while preserving subject identity and source content.

</details>


### [80] [ConfTriage: A Calibration-Aware LLM Triage Framework for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy with Selective Specialist Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10885)
*Md Rabiul Islam,Samir Abdaljalil,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfTriage框架，利用通用大语言模型（LLM）仅阅读结节属性的自然语言描述即可作为肺结节恶性预测的可校准分诊层，对低置信度病例选择性回退到专用深度学习模型。该方法在LIDC-IDRI数据集上取得F1=88.22%、AUC=0.92，76.5%的病例仅依赖零样本LLM推理即可解决。论文提供有限样本组合误差界和Oracle不等式两个理论保证，证明超额风险由LLM概率的L1校准误差控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的肺结节恶性预测依赖基于图像训练的专用深度学习模型，需要大量标注影像数据和任务特异性训练。作者希望探索通用LLM能否仅通过读取标准结节属性的自然语言描述，作为校准的分诊层来补充或替代专用的影像DL模型，从而减少对大量标注影像数据的依赖。

Method: ConfTriage方法基于三个支柱：1）以语言为模态——将标准结节属性转化为自然语言描述供LLM读取；2）以校准为安全机制——对LLM输出概率进行置信度校准；3）选择性专家回退——对低置信度病例转交专用DL模型处理。作者证明了两个理论保证：有限样本组合误差界（给出每阈值操作证书）和Oracle不等式（表明与贝叶斯最优延迟分类器相比，超额风险受LLM概率L1校准误差控制）。此外设计了七种输入的受控消融实验，跨越五个前沿LLM。

Result: 在LIDC-IDRI数据集上，ConfTriage达到F1=88.22%、AUC=0.92，其中76.5%的病例通过零样本LLM推理单独解决，仅不确定病例回退到专家DL模型。七路输入消融实验表明：自然语言描述主导诊断信号，而低级图像统计在诊断上基本无效。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 临床上有意义的诊断信息可以通过结构化放射学描述被捕获，并由校准的LLM用于选择性转诊。该框架为通用LLM预测与专用AI模型在医学决策支持系统中的结合提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Pulmonary nodule malignancy prediction typically depends on image-trained specialist deep learning (DL) models that require substantial annotated imaging data and task-specific training. We investigate whether a generalist large language model (LLM), reading only a faithful natural-language rendering of standard nodule attributes, can serve as a calibrated triage layer. We propose ConfTriage, a confidence-calibrated method built on three pillars: language as the modality, calibration as the safety mechanism, and a selective specialist DL backstop for low-confidence cases. We prove two guarantees: a finite-sample combined-error bound yielding an explicit per-threshold operational certificate, and an oracle inequality showing that excess risk over the Bayes-optimal deferral classifier is controlled by the L1 calibration error of the LLM probability. A controlled seven-way input ablation across five frontier LLMs on LIDC-IDRI shows that natural-language descriptions dominate the diagnostic signal, while low-level image statistics are essentially diagnostically vacuous. ConfTriage achieved an F1 score of 88.22% and an AUC of 0.92, resolving 76.5% of cases using zero-shot LLM inference alone and referring only uncertain cases to the specialist DL backstop. These results demonstrate that clinically meaningful diagnostic information can be captured through structured radiological descriptions and leveraged by calibrated LLMs for selective referral. The framework suggests a practical pathway for combining generalist LLM prediction with specialist AI models in medical decision-support systems. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/rabiul-ai/ConfTriage.

</details>


### [81] [GESTO: Human-Centric Spatio-Temporal Memory for Reasoning in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10886)
*Ermanno Bartoli,Buwei He,Dennis Rotondi,Sebastian Koch,Federico Tombari,Kai O. Arras,Patric Jensfelt,Yixi Cai,Iolanda Leite*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GESTO是一种将持久4D场景图与双层层次结构（原子人-物交互和目标驱动事件）耦合的时空记忆系统，可从RGB-D观测流中自动提取带时间戳的交互、将其锚定到持久场景实体、分组为事件，并利用事件上下文细化不确定的对象关联，支持基于活动的时空推理，在标准基准上取得0.71/0.75/0.70的成绩，并在新的Space2Event和Event2Space查询上分别达到0.73和0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D场景图能保留对象和位置的历史，但缺失活动结构；而活动表示要么未锚定在持久3D场景中，要么依赖外部提供的事件边界和对象关联。需要一种既能持久化场景信息又能捕获交互和活动层次结构的记忆系统。

Method: GESTO由一个持久4D场景图和一个两层活动层次结构组成：(1)自动从RGB-D观测流中提取带时间戳的人-物交互；(2)将这些交互锚定到持久场景实体；(3)将交互按目标分组为事件；(4)利用事件上下文反向细化不确定的对象关联。推理端使用关系感知的工具调用代理查询该记忆系统，实现以活动为中心的时空推理。

Result: 在标准文本、二进制和时间类别上分别取得0.71、0.75和0.70的分数，接近使用真实事件和对象锚定输入的方法；新的Space2Event和Event2Space查询上分别取得0.73和0.75。消融实验表明层次事件结构和上下文感知的锚定细化提供互补收益。

Conclusion: GESTO验证了活动锚定层次化记忆对动态人类环境中回顾性推理的价值，层次事件结构和对齐细化各自贡献互补优势，为机器人持久场景理解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in human environments need memories that capture not only what objects exist and where, but also how people use them over time and how individual interactions compose into goal-directed activities. Existing 4D scene graphs preserve object and place histories but omit activity structure, whereas activity representations are either not grounded in persistent 3D scenes or rely on externally provided event boundaries and object associations. We present GESTO (Grounded Event and Spatio-Temporal memOry), a spatio-temporal memory that couples a persistent 4D scene graph with a two-level hierarchy of atomic human--object interactions and goal-driven events. From an RGB-D observation stream, GESTO automatically extracts timestamped interactions, grounds them to persistent scene entities, groups them into events, and uses event context to refine uncertain object associations. A relation-aware tool-calling agent queries the resulting memory for activity-centric spatio-temporal reasoning. We evaluate GESTO on the reproducible text, binary, and time categories of an existing benchmark, together with 40 new Space2Event and Event2Space queries. GESTO achieves scores of 0.71, 0.75, and 0.70 on the standard categories, approaching a method supplied with ground-truth event and object grounding, while substantially outperforming the same reasoning framework when these inputs are removed. It further achieves 0.73 and 0.75 on Space2Event and Event2Space queries. Ablations show that hierarchical event structure and context-aware grounding refinement provide complementary benefits, supporting activity-grounded hierarchical memory for retrospective reasoning in dynamic human environments.

</details>


### [82] [Sensor-Informed Per-Point Covariance for Structured-Light 3D Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10888)
*Sehoon Tak,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于传感器信息的解析方法，用于在条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）三维重建中构建逐点的3×3协方差场。该方法从实验测量的相位精度及标定的相位-深度/相位-3D映射出发，将秩1相位诱导协方差与全秩的有效补全相结合，为每个点生成反映测量过程各向异性的不确定性模型。实验表明该协方差在G-ICP配准中显著优于各向同性常数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点云协方差模型（各向同性常数或基于局部表面几何推断）未显式反映测量过程。在FPP中，相位噪声通过标定重建传播，产生强烈各向异性的3D不确定性，现有方法无法捕捉这一特性，限制了概率配准、融合与质量评估的精度。

Method: 提出一种传感器感知的一阶方法：利用实验测量的相位精度与标定的相位-深度及相位-3D映射，构建逐点3×3协方差场。公式将秩1的相位诱导协方差与通过拟合横向图像空间扰动尺度得到的有效满秩补全相分离，从而结合测量过程的各向异性信息。

Result: 固定成像条件下重复平面实验显示：主导协方差方向与视射线紧密对齐，主导相位诱导不确定性尺度与标量深度不确定性一致。在G-ICP配准中，所提协方差相比常数各向同性模型显著提升性能，同时提供与传统几何协方差互补的传感器导出不确定性表示。

Conclusion: 传感器导出的逐点协方差场能更准确地反映FPP测量过程中相位噪声传播带来的各向异性3D不确定性，在概率配准任务中优于传统各向同性模型，并与几何基协方差互补，为结构化光3D重建提供更可靠的感知级不确定性建模框架。

Abstract: Per-point uncertainty models are important in structured-light 3D reconstruction for probabilistic registration, fusion, and quality assessment. In practice, however, point-cloud covariances are often modeled as isotropic constants or inferred from local surface geometry and therefore do not explicitly reflect the measurement process. This is a limitation in fringe projection profilometry (FPP), where phase noise propagates through calibrated reconstruction and produces strongly anisotropic 3D uncertainty. This paper presents a sensor-informed first-order method for constructing a per-point 3 x 3 covariance field from experimentally measured phase precision and calibrated phase-to-depth and phase-to-3D mappings. The formulation separates a rank-1 phase-induced covariance from an effective full-rank completion obtained by incorporating fitted lateral image-space perturbation scales. Repeated-plane experiments under fixed imaging conditions show close alignment of the dominant covariance direction with the viewing ray, and consistency between the dominant phase-induced uncertainty scale and scalar depth uncertainty. In G-ICP registration, the proposed covariance substantially improves over a constant isotropic model while providing a sensor-derived uncertainty representation complementary to conventional geometry-based covariances.

</details>


### [83] [VIDS-Seg: Towards Reliable Uncertainty Quantification in Pediatric Cardiac Ultrasound Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10903)
*Paul Fischer,Ece Ozkan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDS-Seg，一种基于变分推断的OOD感知不确定性量化方法，用于医学图像分割中的静默失败检测。在超声心动图左心室分割任务上，模型在成人数据（EchoNet-Dynamic）上训练，零样本测试于儿科数据（EchoNet-Pediatric），结果显示VIDS-Seg在分割精度上与强基线持平，但不确定性估计与分割误差的空间相关性显著更高，并能更可靠地检测婴儿亚组的心功能异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床部署的机器学习模型需要具备"知道自己何时可能失败"的能力，尤其对训练数据中代表性不足的亚组（如儿科患者）。在临床实践中，成人数据训练的模型在儿童上可能静默地性能退化，且无法依赖重新标注儿科数据来微调，因此在推理时检测此类失败是关键的临床需求。

Method: 在VIDS（分布偏移下的变分推断）框架基础上，提出VIDS-Seg：通过摊销变分推断（amortized variational inference）在一个轻量级预测头（prediction head）上实现自适应的、OOD感知的先验，使其对密集图像分割任务可计算。实验设置：以成人队列EchoNet-Dynamic训练，零样本评估于儿科队列EchoNet-Pediatric，覆盖所有年龄分层，并对比温度缩放后的竞争性基线。

Result: 在所有年龄分层上，VIDS-Seg的分割精度与竞争性基线相当，但预测不确定性与分割误差之间的空间对应关系显著更高，该优势在温度缩放后仍然保持。下游应用中，VIDS-Seg产生了更准确和稳定的射血分数估计，并在婴儿亚组中实现更可靠的心功能异常检测。

Conclusion: OOD感知的不确定性量化可以作为一种部署分割模型的实用安全层，无需重新训练或额外标注数据，即可检测代表性不足亚组中的静默失败。

Abstract: Reliable clinical deployment of machine learning requires models that know when they are likely to fail, particularly for subgroups underrepresented in training data. A common case is pediatric care, where models trained on adult cohorts can silently under-perform on children with no indication that something has gone wrong. As retraining with labeled pediatric data is often infeasible, detecting such failures at inference time is a critical clinical need. Building on the VIDS (Variational Inference under Distribution Shifts) framework, we introduce VIDS-Seg, which applies amortized variational inference over a lightweight prediction head to make this adaptive, OOD-aware prior tractable for dense image segmentation. We evaluate VIDS-Seg on left ventricular segmentation in echocardiography, a setting where pediatric anatomy differs systematically from the adult population most segmentation models are trained on, training on an adult cohort (EchoNet-Dynamic) and evaluating zero-shot on a pediatric cohort (EchoNet-Pediatric). Across all age strata, VIDS-Seg matches competitive baselines in segmentation accuracy while producing substantially higher spatial correspondence between predicted uncertainty and segmentation error, an advantage that persists even after applying temperature scaling to all baselines. Downstream, it yields more accurate and stable ejection fraction estimates and more reliable detection of cardiac malfunction in the infant subgroup. Our results indicate that OOD-aware uncertainty quantification can serve as a practical safety layer for deployed segmentation models, enabling detection of silent failures in underrepresented subgroups without retraining or additional labeled data.

</details>


### [84] [Temporally Grounded Compositional Camera Motion Understanding via Geometric Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10932)
*Dazhao Du,Shiyan Du,Jian Liu,Yongjian Yu,Bohai Gu,Tao Han,Hualuo Liu,Eric Liu,Yujia Zhang,Xi Chen,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CamChoreo基准数据集（4,229个真实单镜头视频片段，专家标注时间粒度的复合相机运动，包含20个方向感知标签）与CamDistill方法（将3D几何基础模型知识蒸馏为轻量级相机token，推理时无需运行昂贵几何模型），将相机运动理解从片段级标注推进到时间定位的组合式识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM对相机运动的理解多为片段级标签识别，忽略了真实相机运动的两大特性：同一镜头内运动可变化、多种运动可同时发生。此外，当前视觉编码器侧重语义内容而非相机运动所依赖的几何证据，导致细粒度组合式运动识别困难。

Method: (1)提出CamChoreo基准：4,229个真实单镜头片段，专家标注时间分段，使用20个方向感知标签的紧凑词汇表，近半分段包含复合相机运动；(2)提出CamDistill：训练时将冻结的3D基础模型几何知识蒸馏进轻量级相机token，推理时移除3D模型；(3)同时给出直接注入3D特征的CamInject基线进行对比。

Result: CamDistill在不运行3D教师模型于推理阶段的前提下，达到了与直接特征注入（CamInject）相当的准确率，有效弥补了当前MLLM在细粒度组合式相机运动识别上的不足。

Conclusion: CamChoreo与CamDistill共同将相机运动理解从片段级标注推进到时间定位、组合式识别的新范式，为空间智能和可控视频生成等应用提供了更强的运动理解基础。

Abstract: Understanding camera motion is fundamental to video perception, with applications in spatial intelligence and controllable video generation. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a natural interface for this task, but existing work typically assigns one or more labels to an entire clip. Such clip-level recognition overlooks two defining properties of real camera motion: it can change within a shot, and multiple movements can occur simultaneously. We therefore formulate camera-motion understanding as temporally grounded, compositional recognition, which requires a model to localize motion-consistent intervals and identify every movement active within each interval. We introduce CamChoreo, a benchmark of 4,229 real single-shot clips with expert-annotated temporal segments. Its annotations use a compact vocabulary of 20 direction-aware labels, and nearly half of the segments contain compound camera motion, with multiple movement primitives active simultaneously. Recognizing such fine-grained, compositional motion is hard for current MLLMs, whose visual encoders emphasize semantic content rather than the geometric evidence on which camera motion depends. Directly injecting features from a frozen 3D foundation model addresses this gap, but requires running the expensive geometry model on every input; we refer to this baseline as CamInject. We instead propose CamDistill, which distills the same geometric knowledge into lightweight camera tokens during training and removes the 3D model at inference. CamDistill matches the accuracy of direct feature injection without running the 3D teacher at inference. Together, CamChoreo and CamDistill advance camera-motion understanding from clip-level labeling to temporally grounded, compositional recognition. Project page: https://ddz16.github.io/cammotion.github.io/.

</details>


### [85] [SafeCA: Safe Cross-Attention Localization and Regulation for Text-to-Video Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10933)
*Siyuan Liang,Yupeng Qiu,Junfeng Fang,Rong-Cheng Tu,Jiaxing Huang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeCA 是一种面向文本到视频（T2V）生成模型的架构级防御机制，通过分析交叉注意力特征空间中干净样本与越狱样本之间的累积分离效应和线性可分性趋势，实现注意力掩码、能量归一化、语义空间适配器以及恶意token反传抑制，在保持语义一致性的同时降低约20%的越狱成功率，且几乎不引入推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V生成模型在真实部署中易受越狱攻击而产生有害内容，现有防御方法（输入过滤或重建）存在高计算延迟和语义失真两大问题，亟需一种轻量且不损害语义的防御方案。

Method: 1) 通过实验系统性分析干净样本与越狱样本在交叉注意力特征空间中的差异，首次发现扩散过程中的累积分离效应和线性可分性递增趋势；2) 基于单次推理中从干净提示收集的交叉注意力特征进行注意力稳定性分析，识别关键防御区域和值；3) 通过注意力掩码+能量归一化缓解异常激活，引入轻量级语义空间适配器重定向异常语义流；4) 通过反向传播特征异常信号到输入提示词，检测并抑制潜在恶意token。

Result: 在主流的T2V模型上，SafeCA将越狱成功率降低约20%，推理开销几乎可忽略（仅+0.1s），并保持了良好的文本-视频语义一致性。

Conclusion: SafeCA提供了一种架构级别、可部署的T2V生成模型保护范式，兼顾安全性、效率和语义保真度，适用于商业模型的实际部署场景。

Abstract: Text-to-Video (T2V) generative models are vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in real-world deployment, leading them to produce harmful or inappropriate content. Existing defense approaches mainly rely on input filtering or reconstruction, which not only incur high computational latency but also tend to distort semantics. To address these issues, we experimentally and systematically analyze the differences between clean and jailbreak samples in the cross-attention feature space, revealing for the first time a cumulative separation effect and a progressively increasing trend of linear separability between the two during the diffusion process. Based on this insight, we propose SafeCA, a feature-level defense mechanism for safe cross-attention localization and regularization. Firstly, we identify key defensive regions and values through attention stability analysis using cross-attention features collected from clean prompts within a single inference. Secondly, SafeCA mitigates anomalous activations via attention masking with energy normalization and introduces a lightweight semantic-space adapter to redirect abnormal semantic flows. Furthermore, we detect and suppress potentially malicious tokens by back-propagating feature anomaly signals to the input cue words, thereby enhancing the deployability of the defense in commercial models. Experimental results show that SafeCA reduces the jailbreak success rate by about 20% on mainstream T2V models, adds almost no inference overhead (+0.1s), and maintains good text-video semantic consistency. Overall, SafeCA provides an architecture-level, deployable protection paradigm for T2V generation models.

</details>


### [86] [GS-CPE: Unified 6-Degree-of-Freedom Camera Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10938)
*Huaiyuan Weng,Chul Min Yeum,Su-Min Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-CPE是一个基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的6自由度相机位姿估计框架，采用从粗到精的策略：先通过检索引导的几何方法估计粗略位姿，再通过多尺度优化中的可见性感知掩蔽RGB重投影目标进行位姿精化，并配合自适应重渲染。在室内外多个基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位虽然在场景坐标回归和直接相机位姿回归方面取得了显著进展，但同时在鲁棒泛化性和高精度方面仍然具有挑战性，即现有方法难以兼顾两者。

Method: 提出从粗到精的GS-CPE框架：(1) 粗位姿估计阶段，在3DGS场景表示上通过检索引导的几何位姿估计获得初始位姿；(2) 精化阶段，通过最小化可见性感知的掩蔽RGB重投影损失，在多尺度优化框架中进行位姿精化，并采用自适应重渲染机制。

Result: 在7Scenes、Cambridge Landmarks、FAST-LIVO2数据集以及自定义数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和泛化性方面均一致优于现有方法，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: GS-CPE通过统一基于几何的粗位姿估计与基于3DGS重投影的位姿精化，成功在精度和泛化性两个方面均超越了现有方法，验证了该从粗到精框架在6-DoF相机位姿估计任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in visual localization, from scene coordinate regression to direct camera pose regression, achieving both robust generalization and high accuracy remain challenging. This study introduces GS-CPE (Gaussian Splatting based Camera Pose Estimation), a coarse-to-fine framework for 6-DoF camera pose estimation that unifies geometry-based coarse pose estimation with robust 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) warping based pose refinement. GS-CPE first estimates a coarse pose via retrieval-guided geometric pose estimation on a 3DGS scene representation, then refines it by minimizing a visibility aware masked RGB warping objective in a multi-scale optimization framework, with adaptive re-rendering. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks including 7Scenes, Cambridge Landmarks, FAST-LIVO2 datasets, and a custom dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming in both accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [87] [Mixture-of-Experts-based Entropy Model for Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10947)
*Jonas Brenig,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次将混合专家（MoE）方法引入学习型图像压缩领域，提出基于MoE的熵模型（MoEE），使模型能够根据输入图像选择性激活所需的参数子集，在Kodak数据集上相比VVC实现了-16.85%的BD-Rate提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习型图像压缩方法已超越传统方法的压缩效率，但现有模型对所有输入图像使用相同参数进行编码，缺乏针对不同图像内容的自适应性。MoE方法在NLP和计算机视觉任务中展现了条件计算的优势，启发作者将其应用于图像压缩，以提升模型的适应性和压缩效率。

Method: 提出了基于MoE的熵模型（MoEE），将该方法引入学习型图像压缩框架。MoE机制允许模型根据输入图像的特征，动态地仅激活最相关的参数子集（即专家），而不是对所有输入激活全部参数，从而实现条件计算和更高效的熵建模。

Result: 在Kodak数据集上，所提出的MoEE模型相比VVC实现了-16.85%的BD-Rate提升，表明其压缩效率优于当前最先进的传统编码标准。

Conclusion: 将MoE方法成功应用于学习型图像压缩是可行的且有效的，通过条件计算机制能够提升率失真性能，为未来学习型图像压缩模型的架构设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Learned image compression has seen significant progress in recent years with the development of end-to-end learned models that achieve better compression efficiency than state-of-the-art conventional methods. Recently, Mixture of Experts (MoE) approaches have seen promising results in NLP and computer vision tasks. In this paper, we introduce the MoE approach to learned image compression. We propose a MoE-based Entropy model (MoEE) for learned image compression, allowing the model to selectively activate only the subset of parameters required for the input image. Our model achieves a BD-Rate improvement over VVC of -16.85% on the Kodak dataset.

</details>


### [88] [Multiple Scale Latents for Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10952)
*Jonas Brenig,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种层次化潜在表示方法，通过在不同空间尺度上使用多个潜在表示（每个配备独立的熵模型）来改进学习图像压缩中的熵模型效率，在Kodak数据集上相比VVC实现了17.9%的BD-rate降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数学习图像压缩系统依赖单一潜在表示配合超先验，这种设计限制了其跨空间尺度高效捕捉图像结构的能力，难以充分利用图像在不同尺度上的特征信息。

Method: 采用层次化潜在表示（hierarchical latent representation），在不同尺度上使用多个潜在表示，每个潜在表示配备各自独立的熵模型，从而更好地捕捉潜在表示的空间结构。

Result: 在Kodak数据集上相比VVC实现了17.9%的BD-rate降低，验证了多尺度潜在表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 多尺度层次化潜在表示方法有效提升了图像压缩性能，且该方法与其他学习图像压缩方面的进展是正交的，可以作为一种通用补充模块集成到现有方法中。

Abstract: Most learned image compression systems rely on a single latent representation combined with a hyperprior, which limits their ability to efficiently capture image structure across spatial scales. In this work, we propose a hierarchical latent representation to improve the efficiency of the entropy model. By using multiple latents at different scales, each with its own entropy model, we better capture the spatial structure of the latent representation. Our experiments show that this approach achieves a 17.9% BD-rate reduction over VVC on Kodak, demonstrating the effectiveness of multi-scale latent representations. Furthermore, the approach is orthogonal to other advances in learned image compression, making it a versatile addition to existing methods.

</details>


### [89] [Evidence-Grounded Trustworthy Multimodal Reasoning and Evaluation Benchmark in Complex Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10954)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Bowen Jiang,Xumeng Han,Jiashu Li,Xuehui Yu,Yuling Liu,Guorong Li,Zhenjun Han,Jianbin Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AD2-Bench基准和EGVOR方法，解决多模态大语言模型在恶劣条件下（复杂场景、不利环境）推理可靠性下降的问题。AD2-Bench通过层次化视觉诊断框架，将推理分解为结构化证据链（CoE），识别出两类推理失败原因：空间模糊性和语义不确定性。EGVOR通过显式生成证据原子（空间-语义三元组）替代隐式推理，并结合分层课程训练（从反思监督到强化学习），显著提升恶劣条件下的推理稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在正常场景下表现优异，但在复杂场景和恶劣条件下认知可靠性显著下降。模型常在没有充分视觉证据的情况下进行隐式推断，导致感知与推理脱节。现有面向结果（outcome-oriented）的基准仅评估最终预测，无法诊断底层推理过程中的失败，缺乏对推理失败根本原因的细粒度分析手段。

Method: 1) 提出AD2-Bench基准，引入层次化视觉诊断（Hierarchical Visual Diagnosis）框架，将推理分解为结构化证据链（Chain of Evidence, CoE）以进行细粒度诊断；2) 从概率论视角形式化推理过程，识别两类主要失败原因：空间模糊性（定位错误）和语义不确定性（理解错误）；3) 提出证据接地视觉推理（EGVOR），以明确生成"证据原子"（Evidence Atoms，即结构化的空间-语义三元组）替代隐式推理，强制定位与语义理解紧密对齐；4) 通过从反思监督构建到强化学习的分层课程训练模型，显式奖励降低推理方差的行为。

Result: 大量实验表明，EGVOR在恶劣条件下显著提升了推理稳定性，相比现有方法提供了更鲁棒的可信多模态认知框架。AD2-Bench的细粒度诊断揭示了稳健的多模态推理根本上依赖于准确的证据获取。

Conclusion: 稳健的多模态推理必须建立在精确的证据获取之上，而非隐式推断。通过结构化证据原子显式对齐空间定位与语义理解，并配合分层课程训练策略，可以有效克服恶劣条件下的推理失败，为可信多模态认知提供更可靠的框架。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate impressive performance in benign scenarios, their cognitive reliability deteriorates significantly in complex scenes under adverse conditions. In these settings, models often rely on implicit inference without sufficient visual evidence, leading to a disconnect between perception and reasoning. Meanwhile, existing outcome-oriented benchmarks evaluate only final predictions and fail to diagnose failures in the underlying reasoning process. To address this gap, the authors propose AD2-Bench, which introduces a Hierarchical Visual Diagnosis framework that decomposes reasoning into a structured Chain of Evidence (CoE). This fine-grained diagnosis reveals that robust multimodal reasoning fundamentally depends on accurate evidence acquisition. Building on this perspective, the authors formulate reasoning from a probabilistic viewpoint and identify two primary causes of reasoning failure: Spatial Ambiguity, where models fail to distinguish target objects from background clutter, resulting in localization errors; and Semantic Uncertainty, where degraded visual features lead to incorrect semantic interpretation, resulting in understanding errors. To overcome these evidence deficiencies, they further propose Evidence-grounded Visual Reasoning (EGVOR), which replaces implicit reasoning with the explicit generation of Evidence Atoms - structured spatial-semantic triplets that enforce tight alignment between localization and semantic understanding. The model is trained through a hierarchical curriculum that progresses from reflective supervision construction to reinforcement learning, where reducing reasoning variance is explicitly rewarded. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EGVOR substantially improves reasoning stability under adverse conditions, providing a more robust framework for trustworthy multimodal cognition.

</details>


### [90] [Once Poisoned, Arbitrarily Controlled: A Programmable Backdoor in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10959)
*Tao Lin,Gaojie Jin,Zongxin Liu,Peng Wu,Lijia Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可编程的视觉语言模型（VLM）后门攻击范式，将传统静态的一对一或多对多触发器-目标映射攻击升级为"任意到任意"（any-to-any）字幕控制攻击。通过单次投毒阶段，攻击者可在推理时动态选择训练中未见过的目标字幕语义，并按需合成隐蔽触发器，无需重训VLM即可实现目标字幕的动态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后门攻击被普遍视为静态漏洞，即攻击绑定一个或多个触发器到有限的固定目标集。这种假设严重低估了实际威胁。作者旨在揭示VLM后门攻击中目标选择可在推理阶段任意动态化的安全风险，突破传统攻击范式的能力边界。

Method: 方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 启发式投毒策略：向模型暴露多样化的触发器-字幕配对，鼓励模型学习通用的"触发器即指令"（trigger-as-instruction）规则，而非记忆特定后门模式；(2) 特征空间触发器隐写术：将攻击者指定的目标字幕映射为隐蔽视觉触发器，实现为范数受控的扰动或非语义贴片两种形式。将这些触发器注入任意图像后，中毒VLM生成的输出与所选目标字幕语义对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明该攻击实现了高成功率的任意到任意字幕控制，保持了干净模型的效用，并且在多种经典后门防御下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 该研究证明VLM后门的威胁被系统性低估，攻击者可以在不重训模型的情况下实现任意目标字幕的动态后门控制，且能够规避现有防御机制，对VLM安全研究提出新的挑战。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) backdoors are usually treated as static vulnerabilities: one-to-one and N-to-N attacks bind one or more triggers to a finite set of targets before victim training. This assumption substantially underestimates the threat. We show that a single poisoning phase can implant a programmable backdoor into a VLM, allowing an attacker to choose previously unseen target-caption semantics at inference time and synthesize corresponding stealthy triggers on demand. Unlike fixed-mapping attacks, the proposed any-to-any caption-control paradigm decouples post-training target selection from poisoning, enabling dynamic control of target captions without retraining the VLM. Our method has two components. First, a heuristic poisoning strategy exposes the model to diverse trigger-caption pairs, encouraging it to learn a general trigger-as-instruction rule rather than memorize a specific backdoor pattern. Second, a feature-space trigger steganography method maps any attacker-specified target caption to a stealthy visual trigger, implemented as either a norm-controlled perturbation or a non-semantic patch. Once inserted into arbitrary images, these triggers cause the poisoned VLM to generate outputs semantically aligned with the chosen target caption, even when the target was unseen during poisoning. Extensive experiments show that our attack achieves high any-to-any caption-control success rates, preserves clean model utility, and remains effective under several classical backdoor defenses.

</details>


### [91] [CARE: Confidence-Aware Reasoning for Reliable Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10964)
*Yuetian Du,Yucheng Wang,Zhenyuan Chen,Luyuan Chen,Rongyu Zhang,Jinjian Zhang,Wei Zhou,Zhijie Xu,Ming Kong,Zhan Zhou,Jie Liu,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE框架，一个置信度感知的医学推理框架，通过双阶段流程（可扩展的Medical-CoT合成数据用于监督微调，以及带有置信度感知奖励机制的GRPO强化学习）联合优化医学多模态大语言模型在视觉问答中的诊断准确性和置信度校准，在三个医学VQA基准上同时取得最高诊断准确率和最低期望校准误差与幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过强化微调（RFT）能够产生链式推理，但存在置信度校准错误问题——即模型表达的确定性与实际诊断准确性之间存在系统性差距，这会削弱临床信任。这一置信度失校准问题阻碍了模型在临床决策支持中的可靠应用。

Method: CARE框架采用双阶段流水线：(1) 第一阶段，通过可扩展的Medical-CoT合成方法生成结构化冷启动数据，用于监督微调（SFT）；(2) 第二阶段，使用带新型置信度感知奖励（CAR）机制的GRPO算法，将模型置信度与诊断正确性在奖励信号中绑定，使模型在提升准确性的同时改善校准。

Result: 在三个医学VQA基准测试中，CARE取得最高的诊断准确率，同时获得最低的期望校准误差（ECE）和最低的幻觉率。

Conclusion: CARE为可信赖的临床决策支持建立了基础，通过同时优化准确性和校准性，弥补了RFT后医学MLLM在置信度校准方面的缺陷，为临床环境中安全部署此类模型提供了支撑。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) has enabled medical Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning for visual question answering, yet these models suffer from $\textit{confidence miscalibration}$---a systematic gap between expressed certainty and actual diagnostic accuracy that undermines clinical trust. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$, a $\textbf{C}$onfidence-$\textbf{A}$ware medical $\textbf{RE}$asoning framework that jointly optimizes accuracy and calibration through a dual-stage pipeline. First, a scalable Medical-CoT synthesis provides structured cold-start data for Supervised Fine-Tuning. Second, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel $\textbf{Confidence-Aware Reward (CAR)}$ mechanism ties the model's confidence to diagnostic correctness within the reward signal. Across three Medical VQA benchmarks, $\textbf{CARE}$ achieves the highest diagnostic accuracy while obtaining the lowest Expected Calibration Error and Hallucination Rate, establishing a foundation for trustworthy clinical decision support. Our code is available at https://github.com/anotherbricki/CARE.

</details>


### [92] [A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Music Recognition of String Quartet Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10978)
*Dongmin Kim,Brian Liu,Jose J. Valero-Mas,Dasaem Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OSSQ-OMR，这是首个专用于多声部乐谱识别（OMR）的数据集，基于OpenScore String Quartet语料库构建，包含24,544张系统级图像和98,172张五线谱级图像，来自116首弦乐四重奏乐谱，并提供三种编码格式（LMXE、**kern、ABC）的转录配对，同时附带基准协议和基线实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学音乐识别（OMR）在单声部和钢琴形式乐谱上已取得显著进展，但多声部乐谱转录研究仍十分不足，其主要障碍是缺乏合适的数据集。现有数据集的缺失限制了多声部OMR技术的发展。

Method: 基于OpenScore String Quartet语料库构建数据集，将数字编码乐谱与其来自IMSLP的原始扫描版本配对，实现图像与转录的视觉对齐；数据集包含系统级和五线谱级图像，提供三种编码格式（LMXE、**kern、ABC）的转录；设计了基准协议，并在四种随机乐谱级别的划分（互斥测试集）上评估两个代表性OMR模型。

Result: 基线模型在合成输入上达到低至3.6%的OMR-NED，在扫描输入上达到5.9%；编码和分割选择对结果有显著影响；基于LSTM的基线在扫描输入上性能下降约为基于Transformer的基线的2.6分之一（即LSTM在扫描输入上退化更少）。

Conclusion: OSSQ-OMR填补了多声部OMR数据集的空白，为多声部乐谱识别研究提供了标准化基准；实验结果揭示了编码格式和分割策略对OMR性能的重要影响，并为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Optical music recognition (OMR) transcribes music scores into digital formats. While the field has advanced significantly on monophonic and piano-form scores, multi-part score transcription remains underexplored, largely due to the absence of a suitable dataset. We introduce OpenScore String Quartet for Optical Music Recognition (OSSQ-OMR), the first dataset dedicated to multi-part OMR. Built on the OpenScore String Quartet corpus, OSSQ-OMR pairs digitally encoded scores with their original scanned editions from IMSLP, with all images visually aligned to their transcriptions. The dataset is released with score images at system and staff levels, and paired transcriptions in three encoding formats: Extended Linearized MusicXML (LMXE), **kern, and ABC. In total, OSSQ-OMR contains 24,544 system images and 98,172 staff images drawn from 116 string quartet scores. We accompany the dataset with a benchmark protocol and baseline results from two representative OMR models, evaluated across four random score-level splits with mutually exclusive test sets. Baselines reach OMR-NED as low as 3.6% on synthetic and 5.9% on scanned inputs; results reveal substantial effects of encoding and segmentation choices, with the LSTM-based baseline degrading on scanned inputs roughly 2.6 times less than the Transformer-based baseline.

</details>


### [93] [ThinkAfford: Affordance-Centric Reasoning for Fine-Grained 3D Grounding in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10981)
*Xinrui Lin,Sha Zhang,Shumin Wang,Zenghuan Zhu,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThinkAfford是一种新方法，用于任务驱动的3D关节定位，将高召回率的关节提议生成与指令驱动推理解耦，通过可学习的关节提示生成热图、视觉提示推理和组相对策略优化（GRPO），在SceneFun3D数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D关节定位方法存在两个交织的失败模式：预测或选择的区域可能遗漏目标交互区域或粒度不合适，以及关系型指令下语言推理可能混淆视觉上相似的候选。现有方法对这两类问题没有有效的解耦处理。

Method: ThinkAfford将任务解耦为两个阶段：(1) 关节提议生成模块（APG）使用可学习的关节提示和多层级视觉特征预测交互条件热图，提取可变数量的细粒度提议，无需解析对象或部位名称作为分割提示；(2) 视觉提示关节推理（VPAR）利用完整指令对提议叠加图进行推理，以"先思考后回答"的结构化格式返回标识符。此外，组相对策略优化（GRPO）使用来自提升3D重叠的提议级奖励来对齐VPAR选择与最终3D定位。

Result: 在SceneFun3D验证集上，ThinkAfford达到10.69% AP50和25.46% AP25，优于3D开放词汇和基于视觉语言模型的2D到3D基线。模块级诊断表明APG在25% IoU下达到77.5%召回率，GRPO训练的VPAR在APG覆盖查询上达到72.1%选择准确率（SFT为63.4%）。

Conclusion: 通过将高召回率提议生成与指令推理解耦，并引入策略优化对齐两个阶段，ThinkAfford有效缓解了现有方法的两个失败模式，展示了模块化架构在处理复杂3D关节定位任务上的优势。

Abstract: Task-driven 3D affordance grounding aims to localize the functional region in a cluttered 3D scene that enables an action specified by a natural-language instruction. Existing methods either predict 3D masks directly or construct them by selecting and fusing intermediate 2D/3D regions. However, they remain vulnerable to two intertwined failure modes: the predicted or selected regions may miss the target interaction area or have unsuitable granularity, while language grounding may confuse visually similar alternatives under relational instructions. To this end, we introduce ThinkAfford, which decouples high-recall affordance proposal generation from instruction-grounded reasoning. Specifically, the Affordance Proposal Generation module first uses learnable affordance prompts and multi-level visual features to predict interaction-conditioned heatmaps, extracting a variable number of fine-grained proposals without parsed object or part names as segmentation prompts. Visual-Prompted Affordance Reasoning then reasons over labeled proposal overlays using the full instruction, returning identifiers in a structured "think-then-answer" response. Moreover, Group Relative Policy Optimization uses proposal-level rewards from lifted 3D overlap to align VPAR selection with final 3D grounding. On the SceneFun3D validation split, ThinkAfford achieves 10.69% AP50 and 25.46% AP25 under the official evaluator, outperforming comparable 3D open-vocabulary and vision-language-model-based 2D-to-3D baselines. Module-level diagnostics further show that APG attains 77.5% recall at 25% intersection-over-union, while GRPO-trained VPAR achieves 72.1% selection accuracy on APG-covered queries, compared with 63.4% under supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [94] [PEAK: Precise and Persistent Concept Erasure via k-Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10985)
*Man Jiang,Ouxiang Li,Weibao Xue,Zhenhua Tang,Yuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PEAK提出了一种基于k-Sparse Autoencoders（kSAEs）的精确且持久的概念擦除框架，通过将扩散模型的密集内部表征分解为可解释的稀疏特征，定位目标概念相关的紧凑特征集，并将其注入参数优化中，实现概念擦除。在I2P基准上，NudeNet检测从582降至6，攻击成功率从96.52%降至5.63%，同时保持MS-COCO上的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文本到图像扩散模型中的概念擦除面临双重困境：现有方法在概念相关表征的定位上不够精确，可能导致意外的语义干扰；同时底层概念知识未被完全移除，使得模型容易遭受对抗性恢复攻击。因此需要一种既能精确定位目标概念又能确保持久擦除的方法。

Method: PEAK框架分为两个阶段：(1) 在扩散去噪网络的内部激活上训练kSAE，将密集表征分解为可解释的稀疏特征，通过对比目标与非目标提示引发的稀疏激活，依据激活强度和频率识别紧凑的目标特定特征集；(2) 利用这些定位到的特征进行参数优化，选择性地抑制目标相关激活，同时保持非目标激活与原模型一致，将概念擦除直接嵌入模型参数中。

Result: 在I2P基准上，PEAK将NudeNet检测次数从582降至6，平均攻击成功率（ASR）从96.52%大幅降至5.63%，并在MS-COCO上以接近零的KID保持了通用生成质量，证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PEAK通过kSAE实现精确的概念特征定位和参数级的概念擦除嵌入，在精确性和持久性之间取得了平衡，无需推理时的额外干预即可抵御对抗攻击，为大规模T2I扩散模型的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Erasing concepts from large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models has become increasingly crucial due to the growing concerns over copyright infringement, privacy violations, and offensive content. Existing approaches struggle to achieve both precise and persistent concept erasure: inaccurate localization of concept-related representations may cause unintended semantic interference, while incomplete removal of the underlying concept knowledge allows adversarial recovery. To address this dilemma, we propose PEAK, a \textbf{\textit{precise}} and \textbf{\textit{persistent}} concept erasure framework via k-Sparse Autoencoders (kSAEs). PEAK first trains a kSAE on internal activations of the diffusion denoising network to decompose dense representations into interpretable sparse features. By contrasting sparse activations induced by target and non-target prompts, PEAK identifies a compact set of target-specific features according to both activation strength and frequency. These localized features are then used for parameter optimization, where PEAK selectively suppresses target-related activations while preserving complementary non-target ones towards the original model. This feature-guided optimization embeds concept erasure directly into diffusion parameters, eliminating the need for additional inference-time intervention and facilitating effective persistence against adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PEAK achieves effective and robust concept erasure. On the I2P benchmark, PEAK reduces NudeNet detections from 582 to 6, lowers the average attack success rate (ASR) from 96.52\% to 5.63\%, and preserves general generation quality on MS-COCO with a near-zero KID. Our code and models are available at: https://github.com/manmanTAT/PEAK

</details>


### [95] [Putting Registers to Work: Task Registers for Token Pruning in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10989)
*Hongsen Cao,Mona Jaber,Shanxin Yuan,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练Vision Transformer在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测等不同任务间的Token剪枝策略迁移问题。通过受控探针实验揭示了三类任务在剪枝标准排序、注意力剪枝敏感性和恢复端点选择上的差异，并据此提出任务自适应剪枝（TAP）方法——为每个任务引入独立的任务寄存器，动态排名Token、分配深度维度的精确移除预算并设置密集特征恢复尺度。在ρ=0.5的最终保留率下，TAP-J在ADE20K达到47.0 mIoU（1.30×编码器吞吐量）、在COCO达到53.7 box AP（1.32×吞吐量），同时在ImageNet-1K上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的Token剪枝策略通常针对单一识别流程设计，但预训练Vision Transformer会被复用于空间需求不同的多种任务。作者希望探究剪枝策略的哪些部分能够在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测之间迁移，以解决跨任务剪枝的适配问题。

Method: 首先通过受控探针（controlled probes）系统分析：冻结无剪枝检查点，在单一可剪枝层依次应用一系列无参数缩减准则且不重训练，对比三种任务下的表现差异。基于探针发现，提出Task-Adaptive Pruning (TAP)：摒弃现有寄存器token作为任务无关特征存储的做法，为每个任务引入独立的任务寄存器并仅激活当前任务对应的寄存器。该寄存器的演化状态负责Token排名、跨深度精确移除预算分配和密集特征恢复尺度的设定。此外还提出了联合适配版本TAP-J。

Result: 探针实验发现三个关键差异：(1) 分割和检测对剪枝准则的排名与分类不同；(2) 分类对最早层的注意力式剪枝尤其敏感；(3) 密集任务偏好相反的恢复端点。在ρ=0.5保留率下，TAP-J在ADE20K上达到47.0 mIoU和1.30×编码器吞吐量，在COCO上达到53.7 box AP和1.32×吞吐量，同时在ImageNet-1K上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 剪枝策略不能简单跨任务复用，分类任务与密集预测任务（分割、检测）在剪枝偏好上存在本质差异。TAP通过任务自适应寄存器机制有效适配了不同任务的剪枝需求，在保持分类性能的同时为密集任务带来了显著的吞吐量增益，说明任务感知的Token剪枝设计是预训练ViT高效复用的关键。

Abstract: Token-pruning policies are usually designed for a single recognition pipeline, but pretrained Vision Transformers are reused across tasks with different spatial demands. We ask which parts of a pruning policy transfer across image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection. For each pipeline, controlled probes freeze the no-pruning checkpoint and apply a series of parameter-free reduction criteria at one eligible layer at a time without retraining. The probes reveal three differences: segmentation and detection rank the criteria differently, classification is especially sensitive to attention-based pruning in the earliest layers, and the dense tasks prefer opposite recovery endpoints. These findings motivate Task-Adaptive Pruning (TAP). Existing register tokens serve as task-agnostic storage for feature artifacts. TAP instead introduces one task register per task and activates only the current one. Its evolving state ranks tokens, distributes an exact removal budget over depth, and sets the recovery scale for dense features. At a final keep rate of $ρ=0.5$, our jointly adapted model, TAP-J, reaches $47.0$ mIoU at $1.30\times$ encoder throughput on ADE20K and $53.7$ box AP at $1.32\times$ encoder throughput on COCO while remaining competitive on ImageNet-1K.

</details>


### [96] [HNDiff: Haze-Noise Diffusion for Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10995)
*Jin-Ting He,Fu-Jen Tsai,Yan-Tsung Peng,Min-Hung Chen,Chia-Wen Lin,Yen-Yu Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了HNDiff，一个嵌入大气散射模型作为归纳偏置的扩散去雾框架。通过雾感知噪声调度器在正向过程中联合添加雾和噪声，在反向过程中推导物理一致的去雾去噪过程，并进一步提出Latent HNDiff以增强实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像去雾方法忽略了雾形成的物理机制，从纯高斯噪声重建干净图像，限制了恢复潜力。

Method: 1) 将大气散射模型嵌入扩散框架作为归纳偏置；2) 正向过程提出联合雾-噪声扩散及雾感知噪声调度器，根据雾密度自适应调整噪声注入强度；3) 反向过程推导物理一致的去雾去噪过程；4) 提出Latent HNDiff编译干净潜在先验以增强现有去雾网络。

Result: 在基准数据集上显著提升领先去雾骨干网络的性能，达到最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 将物理模型嵌入扩散过程可以有效增强去雾性能，物理引导的扩散框架相比纯数据驱动方法具有更大的恢复潜力。

Abstract: Existing diffusion-based methods have recently made significant progress in image dehazing. However, they typically neglect the physics of haze formation and reconstruct clean images from pure Gaussian noise, thereby limiting their restoration potential. To address this issue, we propose Haze-Noise Diffusion (HNDiff), a novel diffusion framework that embeds the atmospheric scattering model as an inductive bias. By grounding diffusion in physical principles, HNDiff ensures that the restoration aligns more closely with underlying mechanisms of haze formation. In its forward process, we introduce joint haze-noise diffusion with a haze-aware noise scheduler, which progressively adds both haze and noise to an image. Essentially, the scheduler adapts noise levels according to haze density, meaning that regions with heavier haze receive stronger noise injection to encourage content generation, while clearer regions receive lighter noise to better preserve details, which directly links the forward degradation process with the physics of haze. In the reverse process, we then derive a physically consistent dehazing-denoising process that simultaneously removes haze and noise to restore a clean image in a manner aligned with the forward degradation process. To further enhance practicality, we propose Latent HNDiff, which compiles clean latent priors that can be seamlessly integrated into existing dehazing networks to boost performance. Extensive experiments show that our work significantly improves leading dehazing backbones and achieves state-of-the-art results on benchmark datasets. The project page is available at https://jin-ting-he.github.io/HNDiff .

</details>


### [97] [Watching Synthetic Videos: Aligning Cross-modal Representations with Visual Synthesis for Zero-shot Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11013)
*Liangyu Fu,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Ya Jing,Xuecheng Wu,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WSV框架，通过两阶段训练解决零样本视频描述中的跨模态对齐不足问题。第一阶段利用预训练文生视频模型生成合成视频潜表征，并提出polisher弥合真实与合成视频分布差距；第二阶段设计prompter将精炼后的潜表征作为条件输入GPT-2生成描述。在MSVD、MSR-VTT和VATEX上分别达到B@4=52和CIDEr=95.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本视频描述中，仅文本训练导致训练与推理之间存在跨模态差距，先前方法仅用简单线性变换桥接差距，导致文本-视频表征空间对齐不充分，生成的句子不准确。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架WSV：1) 第一阶段利用预训练的文生视频模型生成合成视频潜表征，并提出polisher弥合真实与合成视频分布之间的差距以增强表征保真度；2) 第二阶段设计prompter，将精炼后的潜表征作为条件来引导GPT-2生成描述。推理时将输入视频通过预训练3D Causal VAE编码，直接输入prompter引导GPT-2输出描述。

Result: 在MSVD、MSR-VTT和VATEX数据集上，所提方法在B@4和CIDEr指标上分别取得了52和95.7的分数。

Conclusion: 通过引入文生视频模型生成合成视频表征并配合polisher精炼，可以有效弥合文本与视频之间的跨模态差距，显著提升零样本视频描述的准确性。

Abstract: Text-only training is a popular paradigm in zero-shot video captioning, where the video distribution is not available to the model during training, leading to a cross-modal gap between the training (text-only) and the inference (video-only). Previous works attempt to bridge the gap through simple linear transformations. However, the inherent gap between text and video makes cross-modal representation space alignment insufficient, resulting in inaccurate sentences. To address this issue, we propose a novel zero-shot video captioning framework (WSV) consisting of two training stages, which first generates corresponding synthetic video latent representations via a pretrained text-to-video generation model. To strengthen the fidelity of the latent representations, we propose a polisher capable of bridging the gap between real and synthetic video distributions. Subsequently, we design a prompter that conditions GPT-2 on the polished latent representations to generate the captions in the second training stage. During inference, an input video is encoded by a pretrained 3D Causal VAE and then fed directly into the prompter, which in turn guides GPT-2 to produce the final caption. Experimental results conducted on MSVD, MSR-VTT, and VATEX datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves scores of 52 and 95.7 on the B@4 and CIDEr metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [98] [R4DSG: Relative 4D Scene Graph Memory for Object-Centric Question Answering in Long Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11017)
*Ke Ma,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Shuai Tan,Yijie Zhong,Hao Chen,Haofen Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R4DSG提出一种用于长时间第一人称视频的相对4D场景图记忆方法。与直接存储原始图序列不同，它将视频转换为紧凑的可查询记忆条目，按时间、地点、持久对象、锚点相对变化和局部交互上下文进行索引。核心思想是分离稳定锚点与动态对象，跨帧维持对象身份一致性，并通过锚点相对转换而非全局对齐世界模型来表示对象状态。在EgoLifeQA的255个对象相关问题子集上，在仅问题检索条件下，整体比EgoRAG-Text提升6.7分，在"何时"类问题上提升12.5分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间第一人称视频是可穿戴AI助手的丰富数据源，但对象中心问题（物品被移到哪里、何时改变状态、为何被重新放置）难以回答，因为基于字幕和转录的记忆很少保留持久对象身份或结构化的空间变化。现有长视频QA方法主要强调时间定位和片段检索，而先前的3D场景图方法假设了比自由运动可穿戴RGB视频（点云、RGB-D、位姿视图、稀疏重建或重建场景）更强大的几何信息。

Method: R4DSG将视频转换为紧凑、可查询的记忆条目，按时间、地点、持久对象、锚点相对变化和局部交互上下文索引。核心设计包括：分离稳定锚点与动态对象、跨帧维护持久对象身份、通过锚点相对转换（而非全局对齐世界模型）表示对象状态。构建于近期仅RGB的前沿技术之上，包括可提示视频分割、时间传播和相对3D提升。

Result: 在EgoLifeQA的255个对象相关问题子集上，在仅问题检索条件下，R4DSG相比EgoRAG-Text整体提升6.7分，在"何时"类问题上提升12.5分，验证了时间组织对象记忆的价值。

Conclusion: 相对4D场景图可作为可穿戴助手、AR系统和具身多媒体智能体的实用记忆基质，其价值在于以锚点相对方式组织时间化对象记忆，而非依赖全局对齐的3D重建。

Abstract: Long-horizon egocentric video is a rich substrate for wearable AI assistants, but object-centric questions such as where an item was moved, when it last changed state, or why it was relocated remain difficult because caption- and transcript-based memories rarely preserve persistent object identity or structured spatial change. Existing long-video QA methods mainly emphasize temporal grounding and clip retrieval, while prior 3D scene-graph methods typically assume stronger geometry than free-motion wearable RGB video provides, including point clouds, RGB-D input, posed views, sparse reconstruction, or reconstructed scenes. R4DSG introduces a relative 4D scene graph memory for long egocentric video. Instead of storing raw graph sequences, R4DSG converts video into compact queryable memory entries indexed by time, place, persistent objects, anchor-relative change, and local interaction context. The main idea is to separate stable anchors from dynamic objects, maintain persistent object identity across frames, and represent object state through anchor-relative transitions rather than a globally aligned world model. Built on recent RGB-only advances in promptable video segmentation, temporal propagation, and relative 3D lifting, the method produces a retrieval-ready memory directly usable for long-horizon question answering. Evaluation on a 255-question object-related subset from EgoLifeQA shows, under question-only retrieval, a 6.7-point overall gain over EgoRAG-Text and a 12.5-point gain on when questions, which highlights the value of temporally organized object memory. These results position relative 4D scene graphs as a practical memory substrate for wearable assistants, AR systems, and embodied multimedia agents. GitHub Page: https://dualtransparency.github.io/R4DSG/.

</details>


### [99] [When Visual Signals Mislead: A Mechanistic Study of Attribute Hallucination in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11024)
*Yufei Zhang,Chenlu Zhan,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISOR框架，通过空图像诊断（VSNR）将VLM的预测分解为视觉logit信号和语言先验信号，发现视觉信号而非语言先验是属性幻觉（attribute hallucination）的主要驱动因素，并据此将失败模式路由到校准、弃权或靶向视觉适应三种修复操作中，在Qwen、InternVL和LLaVA上有效减少属性误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）中的属性幻觉普遍存在且机制尚不清楚。主流解释"语言先验主导"虽推动了先验抑制方法，但该假设从未在属性层面被直接验证。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过机制级诊断提供更准确的归因和更有效的修复。

Method: VISOR包含两个核心组件：(1) VSNR诊断模块，通过将预测分解为视觉logit信号与语言先验信号（基于空图像对照）来精确定位幻觉来源；(2) 基于诊断结果的路由修复机制，根据失败模式将查询分配到校准（处理阈值放置错误）、弃权（处理训练无关的低信噪比）或靶向视觉适应（处理先验抑制无法修正的材料属性失败）。

Result: 在10,791个负地面真值样本、三种VLM家族和三种属性类型上进行评估，视觉信号能强预测误报而语言先验信号接近随机水平。VISOR在Qwen、InternVL和LLaVA上均减少了属性误报，且不依赖先验主导假设。

Conclusion: 属性幻觉主要由视觉信号的质量问题（如低置信度、低信噪比或信号错位）驱动，而非传统认为的语言先验主导。针对不同视觉信号失败模式进行路由修复比统一抑制语言先验更有效。

Abstract: Attribute hallucination---where vision-language models (VLMs) correctly identify an object but mischaracterize its properties---is prevalent yet mechanistically poorly understood. The dominant explanation, language-prior dominance, has motivated prior-suppression methods, but this explanation has not been directly tested at the attribute level. We present VISOR (Visual-Operational Remediation), a unified framework that couples null-image-based diagnosis with routed remediation. Its VSNR diagnostic decomposes each prediction into a visual logit signal and a language-prior signal. Across 10,791 negative-ground-truth samples from three VLM families and three attribute types, the visual signal strongly predicts false positives, whereas the language-prior signal is near chance. VISOR uses this diagnosis to separate two failure modes: low-margin but directionally correct visual signals in color/state attributes, and low-SNR or misaligned visual signals in material attributes. The same diagnosis routes each query to the appropriate operator: calibration for threshold-placement errors, abstention for training-free low-SNR handling, or targeted visual adaptation for material failures that prior suppression cannot correct. Across Qwen, InternVL, and LLaVA, VISOR reduces attribute false positives without relying on the prior-dominance assumption.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-Level Evidence Aggregation for Robust Facial Phenotype Retrieval in Rare Genetic Disorder Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11037)
*Alexander Hustinx,Carolin Kaffiné,Behnam Javanmardi,Tzung-Chien Hsieh,Peter Krawitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推理时的多层级证据聚合框架，通过结合患者图像嵌入聚合、患者加权疾病质心和混合个体-质心评分，在不修改底层GestaltMatcher-Arc编码器的情况下显著提升面部表型检索的罕见遗传病分诊性能。在GMDB v1.1.4上，Top-1准确率在常见疾病子集从38.52%提升至48.82%，罕见疾病子集从19.38%提升至23.79%，多图像子集提升更为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GestaltMatcher的检索框架采用逐点（pointwise）比较方式，即每个测试图像与图库中的单个图像进行比对。这种点对点公式未能充分利用可用证据，因为同一患者可能有多张图像，同一疾病也可能由多个已确诊的图库患者代表，导致证据利用不充分。

Method: 提出推理时的多层级证据聚合框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 嵌入级患者聚合——聚合同一患者的多张图像嵌入；(2) 患者加权疾病质心——为每种疾病构建加权质心表示；(3) 混合个体-质心评分——结合测试患者观测、疾病级图库证据和局部最近邻证据进行综合打分。该框架完全在推理阶段运行，无需重训练底层编码器。

Result: 在GMDB v1.1.4上评估，多层级证据聚合在所有评估子集上均提升了每疾病平均Top-N检索准确率。GMDB-Freq的Top-1准确率从38.52%提升至48.82%，GMDB-Rare从19.38%提升至23.79%；多图像子集方面，GMDB-Multi-Freq从46.12%提升至60.94%，GMDB-Multi-Rare从18.54%提升至26.71%。

Conclusion: 推理时聚合可以在无需重训练编码器的情况下改进下一代面部表型检索，支持从孤立的单图像匹配向患者与疾病证据的多层级聚合转变，适用于罕见疾病的优先排序。

Abstract: AI-assisted facial phenotyping supports rare genetic disorder prioritization by retrieving visually similar diagnosed cases from facial image reference databases such as the GestaltMatcher Database (GMDB). Existing GestaltMatcher-based retrieval frameworks compare each test image with individual gallery images in a facial phenotype embedding space. However, this pointwise formulation does not fully exploit available evidence, because patients may have multiple images and disorders may be represented by multiple diagnosed gallery patients.
  We propose an inference-time multi-level evidence aggregation framework that improves facial phenotype retrieval without modifying the underlying GestaltMatcher-Arc encoder. The framework combines embedding-level patient aggregation of multiple images from the same individual, patient-weighted disorder centroids, and hybrid individual-centroid scoring to integrate test-patient observations, disorder-level gallery evidence, and local nearest-neighbor evidence.
  We evaluated the approach on GMDB v1.1.4 across disorders represented during training (GMDB-Freq), unseen disorders (GMDB-Rare), and multi-image patient subsets, using a unified gallery containing both GMDB-Freq and GMDB-Rare disorders. Multi-level evidence aggregation improved mean per-disorder top-$N$ retrieval accuracy across all evaluation subsets. Top-1 accuracy increased from 38.52% to 48.82% on GMDB-Freq and from 19.38% to 23.79% on GMDB-Rare. On multi-image subsets, top-1 accuracy increased from 46.12% to 60.94% on GMDB-Multi-Freq and from 18.54% to 26.71% on GMDB-Multi-Rare.
  These findings show that inference-time aggregation can improve next-generation facial phenotype retrieval without retraining the encoder, supporting a shift from isolated single-image matching toward multi-level aggregation of patient and disorder evidence for rare-disorder prioritization.

</details>


### [101] [3D Weighted Geometric Graph Neural Networks for Sheep Facial Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11050)
*Alam Noor,Luis Almeida,Mohamed Daoudi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-SPFES系统，利用单目RGB相机通过VideoDepthAnything估计深度，将绵羊疼痛面部表情量表（SPFES）中各面部标志点集成到3D欧几里得空间，构建加权几何图神经网络（WG-GNN）进行疼痛评分，输出0-100%的归一化疼痛分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习系统主要局限于2D单图像域，将面部视为单维表示，忽略了绵羊的3D解剖结构及SPFES临床量表中固有的跨标志点空间关系，因此需要引入3D几何信息来提升疼痛评估的准确性。

Method: 提出3D-SPFES系统：1）使用VideoDepthAnything从单目RGB相机估计深度，将SPFES面部标志点（耳、眼、鼻等）映射到3D欧几里得空间；2）每个标志点节点包含3D空间坐标、估计表面法线和面部属性类别嵌入的特征向量；3）边权重基于欧氏距离与表面共面性的聚合度量；4）采用加权几何图神经网络（WG-GNN），通过K=3层几何感知消息传递及缩放点积注意力机制增强解剖相关节点间的消息传递；5）节点嵌入聚为O=3个疼痛水平簇，集成至0-100%范围的归一化疼痛评分（NPS）。

Result: 系统输出三个疼痛水平簇的节点嵌入，并整合为置信度加权的、源自SPFES的归一化疼痛评分（NPS），范围为0-100%。

Conclusion: 3D-SPFES通过单目深度感知的几何图神经网络，无需专用深度硬件即可实现基于3D解剖结构的绵羊疼痛面部表情评估，弥补了传统2D方法对空间结构关系缺失的不足。

Abstract: Deep learning systems perform mainly within the 2D for a single image domain and take the face as a single-dimension representation, losing sight of the 3D anatomy of sheep and cross-landmark spatial relationships that are intrinsic to the clinically proven Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES). This paper presents the \textbf{3D Sheep Pain Facial Expression System (3D-SPFES)}, a novel, monocular depth-aware geometric graph neural network system that integrates each SPFES facial landmark, such as the ears, eyes, and nose, into 3D Euclidean space estimated from a single RGB camera by using VideoDepthAnything, thus preventing the need for specialized depth hardware.
